   248	    const files = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).files) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).files as unknown[]) : [];
   249	    for (const file of files) {
   250	      const readPath = normalizePath(asRecord(file).path);
   251	      if (!readPath) {
   252	        continue;
   253	      }
   254	      if (changedPaths.has(readPath)) {
   255	        state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);
   256	      } else {
   257	        state.readCrossFilePaths.add(readPath);
   258	      }
   259	    }
   260	    return;
   260	    return;
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  if (action.tool === 'search_code') {
   264	    state.searchUsed = true;
   265	    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).matches) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).matches as unknown[]) : [];
   266	    state.searchMatchedChangedPath = matches.some((match) => {
   267	      const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   268	      return Boolean(matchPath && changedPaths.has(matchPath));
   269	    });
   270	  }
   271	}
   272	
   273	function collectMissingEvidenceRequirements(input: {
   274	  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
   275	  changedPaths: string[];
   276	  requiresCrossFileEvidence: boolean;
   277	}): string[] {
   278	  const missing: string[] = [];
   279	  const sensitiveChangedPaths = input.changedPaths.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   280	
   281	  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.diffSummaryUsed) {
   282	    missing.push('Run diff_summary at least once before completing analysis.');
   283	  }
   284	
   285	  const requiredReadCoverage =
   286	    input.changedPaths.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
   287	      ? input.changedPaths.length
   288	      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, input.changedPaths.length);
   289	  if (input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size < requiredReadCoverage) {
   290	    missing.push(
   291	      `Read at least ${requiredReadCoverage} changed file(s) directly with read_file (currently ${input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size}).`
   292	    );
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  const missingSensitivePaths = sensitiveChangedPaths.filter((path) => !input.evidence.readChangedPaths.has(path));
   296	  if (missingSensitivePaths.length > 0) {
   297	    missing.push(`Read sensitive changed file(s) directly: ${missingSensitivePaths.join(', ')}.`);
   298	  }
   299	
   300	  if (input.requiresCrossFileEvidence && input.evidence.readCrossFilePaths.size === 0) {
   301	    missing.push('Read at least one non-changed file that defines or handles an integration boundary touched by the diff.');
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.searchUsed) {
   305	    missing.push('Run search_code at least once before completing analysis.');
   306	  }
   307	
   308	  return missing;
   309	}
   310	
   311	function selectDeterministicReadPaths(changedPaths: string[]): string[] {
   312	  const deduped = Array.from(new Set(changedPaths));
   313	  const sensitive = deduped.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   314	  const requiredCount =
   315	    deduped.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
   316	      ? deduped.length
   317	      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, deduped.length);
   318	  const selected = [...sensitive];
   319	  for (const path of deduped) {
   320	    if (selected.length >= Math.max(requiredCount, sensitive.length)) {
   321	      break;
   322	    }
   323	    if (!selected.includes(path)) {
   324	      selected.push(path);
   325	    }
   326	  }
   327	  return selected.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_READ_PATHS);
   328	}
   329	
   330	function pathStem(path: string): string | null {
   331	  const fileName = path.split('/').pop() ?? '';
   332	  const stem = fileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '').trim();
   333	  if (stem.length < DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_MIN_QUERY_LENGTH) {
   334	    return null;
   335	  }
   336	  return stem;
   337	}
   338	
   339	function maybeAddSearchQuery(queries: string[], seen: Set<string>, candidate: string): void {
   340	  const normalized = candidate.trim();
   341	  if (normalized.length < DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_MIN_QUERY_LENGTH) {
   342	    return;
   343	  }
   344	  if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(normalized)) {
   345	    return;
   346	  }
   347	  const lowered = normalized.toLowerCase();
   348	  if (IGNORABLE_SEARCH_TOKENS.has(lowered)) {
   349	    return;
   350	  }
   351	  if (seen.has(lowered)) {
   352	    return;
   353	  }
   354	  seen.add(lowered);
   355	  queries.push(normalized);
   356	}
   357	
   358	type DeterministicSearchKind = 'api_route' | 'imported_symbol' | 'callback' | 'dependency';
   359	
   360	interface DeterministicSearchQuery {
   361	  query: string;
   362	  sourcePath: string;
   363	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind;
   364	}
   365	
   366	function maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(
   367	  queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[],
   368	  seen: Set<string>,
   369	  candidate: string,
   370	  sourcePath: string,
   371	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind
   372	): void {
   373	  const tempQueries: string[] = [];
   374	  const beforeSize = seen.size;
   375	  maybeAddSearchQuery(tempQueries, seen, candidate);
   376	  if (seen.size > beforeSize && tempQueries.length > 0) {
   377	    queries.push({ query: tempQueries[0], sourcePath, kind });
   378	  }
   379	}
   380	
   381	function extractDeterministicSearchQueries(changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   382	  const queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   383	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   384	  const sensitiveFiles = changedFiles.filter((file) => isSensitiveChangedPath(file.path));
   385	  for (const file of sensitiveFiles) {
   386	    const importMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bimport\s+(?:type\s+)?(?:\{([^}]+)\}|([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*))/g);
   387	    for (const match of importMatches) {
   388	      const importedBlock = match[1] ?? match[2] ?? '';
   389	      for (const item of importedBlock.split(',')) {
   390	        const cleaned = item.replace(/\bas\b.+$/i, '').trim();
   391	        maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, cleaned, file.path, 'dependency');
   392	        if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   393	          return queries;
   394	        }
   395	      }
   396	    }
   397	
   398	    const callMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\b([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,})\s*\(/g);
   399	    for (const match of callMatches) {
   400	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, match[1] ?? '', file.path, 'dependency');
   401	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   402	        return queries;
   403	      }
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    const stem = pathStem(file.path);
   407	    if (stem) {
   408	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, stem, file.path, 'dependency');
   409	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   410	        return queries;
   411	      }
   412	    }
   413	  }
   414	
   415	  if (queries.length === 0) {
   416	    const fallbackStem = changedFiles.map((file) => pathStem(file.path)).find(Boolean);
   417	    if (fallbackStem) {
   418	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, fallbackStem, changedFiles[0]?.path ?? '.', 'dependency');
   419	    }
   420	  }
   421	
   422	  return queries.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES);
   423	}
   424	
   425	export function extractDeterministicSearchQueriesForTests(
   426	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']
   427	): string[] {
   428	  return extractDeterministicSearchQueries(changedFiles).map((entry) => entry.query);
   429	}
   430	
   431	function extractStaticApiTailQueries(content: string): string[] {
   432	  const queries: string[] = [];
   433	  const lines = content.split('\n');
   434	  for (const line of lines) {
   435	    if (!line.includes('/api/')) {
   436	      continue;
   437	    }
   438	    const segments = Array.from(line.matchAll(/\/([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,})(?=(?:\/|\$|['"`)}\],; ]))/g))
   439	      .map((match) => match[1] ?? '')
   440	      .filter(Boolean);
   441	    for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   442	      const segment = segments[index];
   443	      if (segment === 'api' || segment === 'review' || segment === 'reviews' || segment === 'workspace' || segment === 'workspaces') {
   444	        continue;
   445	      }
   446	      queries.push(`/${segment}`);
   447	      break;
   448	    }
   449	  }
   450	  return queries;
   451	}
   452	
   453	function extractIntegrationSearchQueries(changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   454	  const queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   455	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   456	
   457	  for (const file of changedFiles) {
   458	    for (const query of extractStaticApiTailQueries(file.content)) {
   459	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, query, file.path, 'api_route');
   460	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   461	        return queries;
   462	      }
   463	    }
   464	
   465	    const importedNames = new Set<string>();
   466	    const importMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bimport\s+(?:type\s+)?(?:\{([^}]+)\}|([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*))/g);
   467	    for (const match of importMatches) {
   468	      const importedBlock = match[1] ?? match[2] ?? '';
   469	      for (const item of importedBlock.split(',')) {
   470	        const cleaned = item.replace(/\bas\b.+$/i, '').trim();
   471	        if (!cleaned || !INTEGRATION_SYMBOL_KEYWORDS.test(cleaned)) {
   472	          continue;
   473	        }
   474	        importedNames.add(cleaned);
   475	      }
   476	    }
   477	    let importedQueriesAdded = 0;
   478	    for (const importedName of importedNames) {
   479	      if (!new RegExp(`\\b${importedName}\\s*\\(`).test(file.content)) {
   480	        continue;
   481	      }
   482	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, importedName, file.path, 'imported_symbol');
   483	      importedQueriesAdded += 1;
   484	      if (
   485	        queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES ||
   486	        importedQueriesAdded >= MAX_INTEGRATION_IMPORTED_SYMBOL_QUERIES_PER_FILE
   487	      ) {
   488	        break;
   489	      }
   490	    }
   491	    if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   492	      return queries;
   493	    }
   494	
   495	    let callbackQueriesAdded = 0;
   496	    const callbackMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bon[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,}\b/g);
   497	    for (const match of callbackMatches) {
   498	      const callbackName = match[0] ?? '';
   499	      if (!INTEGRATION_CALLBACK_PATTERN.test(callbackName)) {
   500	        continue;
   501	      }
   502	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, callbackName, file.path, 'callback');
   503	      callbackQueriesAdded += 1;
   504	      if (
   505	        queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES ||
   506	        callbackQueriesAdded >= MAX_INTEGRATION_CALLBACK_QUERIES_PER_FILE
   507	      ) {
   508	        break;
   509	      }
   510	    }
   511	    if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   512	      return queries;
   513	    }
   514	  }
   515	
   516	  return queries.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES);
   517	}
   518	
   519	export function extractIntegrationSearchQueriesForTests(
   520	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']
   521	): string[] {
   522	  return extractIntegrationSearchQueries(changedFiles).map((entry) => entry.query);
   523	}
   524	
   525	function topLevelPackage(path: string): string {
   526	  return path.split('/').slice(0, 2).join('/');
   527	}
   528	
   529	function scoreSearchMatch(input: {
   530	  sourcePath: string;
   531	  candidatePath: string;
   532	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind;
   533	  query: string;
   534	}): number {
   535	  let score = pathDistanceScore(input.sourcePath, input.candidatePath);
   536	  if (input.kind === 'api_route') {
   537	    if (/\/src\/api\//.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   538	      score -= 6;
   539	    }
   540	    if (/packages\/worker\//.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   541	      score -= 3;
   542	    }
   543	    if (/recover|fail|retry|events/i.test(input.query) && /recover|fail|retry|events/i.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   544	      score -= 3;
   545	    }
   546	    if (/reviews/i.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   547	      score -= 1;
   548	    }
   549	    return score;
   550	  }
   551	
   552	  if (input.kind === 'imported_symbol' || input.kind === 'callback') {
   553	    if (topLevelPackage(input.candidatePath) === topLevelPackage(input.sourcePath)) {
   554	      score -= 4;
   555	    }
   556	    if (input.candidatePath.split('/').slice(0, 3).join('/') === input.sourcePath.split('/').slice(0, 3).join('/')) {
   557	      score -= 2;
   558	    }
   559	  }
   560	
   560	
   561	  return score;
   562	}
   563	
   564	function combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   565	  primary: DeterministicSearchQuery[],
   566	  secondary: DeterministicSearchQuery[]
   567	): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   568	  const combined: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   569	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   570	  for (const query of [...primary, ...secondary]) {
   571	    const lowered = query.query.trim().toLowerCase();
   572	    if (!lowered || seen.has(lowered)) {
   573	      continue;
   574	    }
   575	    seen.add(lowered);
   576	    combined.push(query);
   577	    if (combined.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   578	      break;
   579	    }
   580	  }
   581	  return combined;
   582	}
   583	
   584	function pathDistanceScore(sourcePath: string, candidatePath: string): number {
   585	  const sourceSegments = sourcePath.split('/');
   586	  const candidateSegments = candidatePath.split('/');
   587	  let shared = 0;
   588	  while (
   589	    shared < sourceSegments.length &&
   590	    shared < candidateSegments.length &&
   591	    sourceSegments[shared] === candidateSegments[shared]
   592	  ) {
   593	    shared += 1;
   594	  }
   595	  return (sourceSegments.length - shared) + (candidateSegments.length - shared);
   596	}
   597	
   598	export function computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(maxSteps: number): { deterministicMaxSteps: number; providerMaxSteps: number } {
   599	  const providerMaxSteps = Math.max(1, maxSteps - Math.max(0, maxSteps - MIN_PROVIDER_REASONING_STEPS));
   600	  const deterministicMaxSteps = Math.max(0, maxSteps - providerMaxSteps);
   601	  return { deterministicMaxSteps, providerMaxSteps };
   602	}
   603	
   604	async function runDeterministicEvidenceCollection(input: {
   605	  sandbox: SandboxClient;
   606	  policy: ReviewCommandPolicy;
   607	  maxFileBytes: number;
   608	  authoritativeDiffSnapshot: unknown;
   609	  changedPaths: string[];
   610	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles'];
   611	  changedPathsSet: Set<string>;
   612	  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
   613	  usedTools: string[];
   614	  history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[];
   615	  maxDeterministicSteps: number;
   616	  onLifecycleEvent?: (eventType: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) => void | Promise<void>;
   617	}): Promise<number> {
   618	  const deterministicTools: Array<Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>> = [
   619	    { type: 'tool', tool: 'diff_summary', args: { maxBytes: 64_000 } },
   620	    ...selectDeterministicReadPaths(input.changedPaths).map((path) => ({
   621	      type: 'tool' as const,
   622	      tool: 'read_file' as const,
   623	      args: { path, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 48_000) },
   624	    })),
   625	  ];
   626	  const followUpReadPaths: string[] = [];
   627	
   628	  let deterministicStep = 0;
   629	  for (const action of deterministicTools) {
   630	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   631	      break;
   632	    }
   633	    deterministicStep += 1;
   634	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   635	      input.sandbox,
   636	      action,
   637	      input.policy,
   638	      input.maxFileBytes,
   639	      action.tool === 'diff_summary' ? input.authoritativeDiffSnapshot : undefined
   640	    );
   641	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   642	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   643	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   644	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   645	        step: deterministicStep,
   646	        tool: action.tool,
   647	        deterministic: true,
   648	      });
   649	    }
   650	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   651	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   652	  }
   653	
   654	  const deterministicSearchQueries = combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   655	    extractIntegrationSearchQueries(input.changedFiles),
   656	    extractDeterministicSearchQueries(input.changedFiles)
   657	  );
   658	  for (const searchQuery of deterministicSearchQueries) {
   659	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   660	      break;
   661	    }
   662	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   663	      type: 'tool',
   664	      tool: 'search_code',
   665	      args: {
   666	        query: searchQuery.query,
   667	        path: '.',
   668	        maxResults: 20,
   669	        maxBytesPerFile: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 16_000),
   670	      },
   671	    };
   672	    deterministicStep += 1;
   673	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   674	      input.sandbox,
   675	      action,
   676	      input.policy,
   677	      input.maxFileBytes,
   678	    );
   679	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   680	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   681	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   682	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   683	        step: deterministicStep,
   684	        tool: action.tool,
   685	        deterministic: true,
   686	        query: searchQuery.query,
   687	      });
   688	    }
   689	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   690	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   691	
   692	    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(output.result).matches) ? (asRecord(output.result).matches as unknown[]) : [];
   693	    const rankedMatches = matches
   694	      .map((match) => {
   695	        const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   696	        return matchPath
   697	          ? {
   698	              matchPath,
   699	              score: scoreSearchMatch({
   700	                sourcePath: searchQuery.sourcePath,
   701	                candidatePath: matchPath,
   702	                kind: searchQuery.kind,
   703	                query: searchQuery.query,
   704	              }),
   705	            }
   706	          : null;
   707	      })
   708	      .filter((match): match is { matchPath: string; score: number } => Boolean(match))
   709	      .sort((left, right) => left.score - right.score);
   710	    for (const match of rankedMatches) {
   711	      const matchPath = match.matchPath;
   712	      if (input.changedPathsSet.has(matchPath) || followUpReadPaths.includes(matchPath)) {
   713	        continue;
   714	      }
   715	      followUpReadPaths.push(matchPath);
   716	      if (followUpReadPaths.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_CROSS_FILE_READS) {
   717	        break;
   718	      }
   719	    }
   720	  }
   721	
   722	  for (const path of followUpReadPaths) {
   723	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   724	      break;
   725	    }
   726	    const batchPaths = [path, ...followUpReadPaths.filter((candidate) => candidate !== path)].slice(0, 2);
   727	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   728	      type: 'tool',
   729	      tool: 'read_batch',
   730	      args: { paths: batchPaths, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 32_000) },
   731	    };
   732	    deterministicStep += 1;
   733	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   734	      input.sandbox,
   735	      action,
   736	      input.policy,
   737	      input.maxFileBytes,
   738	    );
   739	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   740	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   741	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   742	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   743	        step: deterministicStep,
   744	        tool: action.tool,
   745	        deterministic: true,
   746	        crossFile: true,
   747	      });
   748	    }
   749	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   750	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   751	    break;
   752	  }
   753	
   754	  return deterministicStep;
   755	}
   756	
   757	/**
   758	 * Hydrates a temporary sandbox from a stored source bundle and reads a bounded set of files
   759	 * through the same read-only tool path used by review analysis.
   760	 */
   761	export async function readWorkspaceFilesFromSourceBundle(
   762	  env: Env,
   763	  input: {
   764	    sourceBundleKey: string;
   765	    sandboxId: string;
   766	    paths: string[];
   767	    maxFileBytes?: number;
   768	  }
   769	): Promise<ReviewSourceFileReadResult[]> {
   770	  const maxFileBytes = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_FILE_BYTES, DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_FILE_BYTES, 1_024, 200_000);
   771	  const effectiveMaxFileBytes =
   772	    typeof input.maxFileBytes === 'number' && Number.isFinite(input.maxFileBytes)
   773	      ? Math.max(1_024, Math.min(maxFileBytes, Math.floor(input.maxFileBytes)))
   774	      : maxFileBytes;
   775	
   776	  if (!env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS && !env.SOURCE_BUNDLES) {
   777	    throw new Error('WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS or SOURCE_BUNDLES binding is required for review context assembly');
   778	  }
   779	  const bundle =
   780	    (env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS ? await env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null) ??
   781	    (env.SOURCE_BUNDLES ? await env.SOURCE_BUNDLES.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null);
   782	  if (!bundle) {
   783	    throw new Error(`Review source bundle not found: ${input.sourceBundleKey}`);
   784	  }
   785	
   786	  const sandbox = await resolveReviewSandbox(env, input.sandboxId);

   598	export function computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(maxSteps: number): { deterministicMaxSteps: number; providerMaxSteps: number } {
   599	  const providerMaxSteps = Math.max(1, maxSteps - Math.max(0, maxSteps - MIN_PROVIDER_REASONING_STEPS));
   600	  const deterministicMaxSteps = Math.max(0, maxSteps - providerMaxSteps);
   601	  return { deterministicMaxSteps, providerMaxSteps };
   602	}

    95	                          endLine: { anyOf: [{ type: 'number' }, { type: 'null' }] },
    96	                        },
    97	                        required: ['filePath', 'startLine', 'endLine'],
    98	                      },

   107	                },

   119	  },
   120	} as const;
   121	
   122	function parseOpenRouterContent(payload: unknown): string {
   123	  const record = asRecord(payload);
   124	  const choices = Array.isArray(record.choices) ? record.choices : [];
   125	  const first = asRecord(choices[0]);
   126	  const message = asRecord(first.message);
   127	  const content = message.content;
   128	
   129	  if (typeof content === 'string') {
   130	    return content.trim();
   131	  }
   132	
   133	  if (Array.isArray(content)) {
   134	    return content
   135	      .map((part) => {
   136	        const entry = asRecord(part);
   137	        return typeof entry.text === 'string' ? entry.text : '';
   138	      })
   139	      .join('')
   140	      .trim();
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  return '';
   144	}
   145	
   146	function buildHistoryForPrompt(history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[]): string {
   147	  const serialized = JSON.stringify(history);
   148	  return clampText(serialized, REVIEW_HISTORY_MAX_BYTES).text;
   149	}
   150	
   151	function buildOpenRouterStepPrompt(input: {
   152	  prompt: string;
   153	  maxSteps: number;
   154	  step: number;
   155	  history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[];
   156	  forceComplete: boolean;
   157	}): string {
   158	  return [
   159	    input.prompt,
   160	    '',
   161	    'Agent loop instructions:',
   162	    `- Current step: ${input.step} of ${input.maxSteps}.`,
   163	    '- You are inside Nimbus\'s internal review harness loop.',
   164	    '- Decide whether to request ONE read-only tool call or finish with complete structured review JSON.',
   165	    '- Tools available: list_files, read_file, read_batch, diff_summary, search_code.',
   166	    '- Never request write_file or run_command.',
   167	    input.forceComplete
   168	      ? '- HARD REQUIREMENT: return type="complete" now. Do not request any tool.'
   169	      : '- If critical evidence is still missing, request one targeted tool call; otherwise finish.',
   170	    '- For type="complete", set finalOutput to a JSON object payload for ReviewAnalysisOutputV2.',
   171	    '- For type="complete", set summary to null or a short plain-text note (never structured payload).',
   172	    '- Return ONLY a valid action object matching the response schema.',
   173	    '',
   174	    `Prior loop history JSON: ${buildHistoryForPrompt(input.history)}`,
   175	  ].join('\n');
   176	}
   177	
   178	export class OpenRouterReviewProvider implements ReviewAgentProvider {
   179	  constructor(
   180	    private readonly apiKey: string,
   181	    private readonly validateAction: (action: unknown) => ReviewAgentAction,
   182	    private readonly httpReferer: string,
   183	    private readonly xTitle: string
   184	  ) {}
   185	
   186	  async next(input: {
   187	    prompt: string;
   188	    model: string;
   189	    reasoningEffort?: 'minimal' | 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
   190	    maxSteps: number;
   191	    step: number;
   192	    history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[];
   193	    forceComplete?: boolean;
   194	  }): Promise<ReviewAgentAction> {
   195	    const forceComplete = input.forceComplete === true;
   196	
   197	    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
   198	      const controller = new AbortController();
   199	      let timeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
   200	
   201	      try {
   202	        const requestPromise = fetch(OPENROUTER_CHAT_COMPLETIONS_URL, {
   203	          method: 'POST',
   204	          headers: {
   205	            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   206	            Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
   207	            'HTTP-Referer': this.httpReferer,
   208	            'X-Title': this.xTitle,
   209	          },
   210	          body: JSON.stringify({
   211	            model: input.model,
   212	            ...(input.reasoningEffort ? { reasoning: { effort: input.reasoningEffort } } : {}),
   213	            response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: reviewAgentActionJsonSchema },
   214	            plugins: [{ id: 'response-healing' }],
   215	            messages: [
   216	              {
   217	                role: 'user',
   218	                content: buildOpenRouterStepPrompt({
   219	                  prompt: input.prompt,
   220	                  maxSteps: input.maxSteps,
   221	                  step: input.step,
   222	                  history: input.history,
   223	                  forceComplete,
   224	                }),
   225	              },
   226	            ],
   227	          }),
   228	          signal: controller.signal,
   229	        });
   230	
   231	        const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
   232	          timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
   233	            controller.abort('review_provider_timeout');
   234	            reject(new Error(`Review analysis provider request timed out after ${Math.floor(REVIEW_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)} seconds`));
   235	          }, REVIEW_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS);
   236	        });
   237	
   238	        const response = await Promise.race([requestPromise, timeoutPromise]);
   239	        const bodyText = await response.text();
   240	        if (!response.ok) {
   241	          const providerErrorBody = sanitizeErrorMessage(bodyText, { openrouterApiKey: this.apiKey });
   242	          if (isMissingOpenRouterApiKeyError(providerErrorBody) || response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
   243	            throw new Error('missing_openrouter_api_key: valid OpenRouter API key is required for review analysis');
   244	          }
   245	          if (isWorkerToWorkerFetchRestriction(response.status, providerErrorBody)) {
   246	            throw new Error(
   247	              `Review analysis provider request blocked by Cloudflare Worker-to-Worker fetch restriction (error code 1042). Enable the 'global_fetch_strictly_public' compatibility flag on this worker or switch to a service binding.${providerErrorBody ? ` Provider response: ${providerErrorBody}` : ''}`
   248	            );
   249	          }
   250	
   251	          const transientStatus = response.status >= 500 || response.status === 429;
   252	          const statusError = new Error(
   253	            transientStatus
   254	              ? `Review analysis provider temporarily unavailable (status ${response.status})${providerErrorBody ? `: ${providerErrorBody}` : ''}`
   255	              : `Review analysis provider request failed with status ${response.status}${providerErrorBody ? `: ${providerErrorBody}` : ''}`
   256	          );
   257	
   258	          if (transientStatus && attempt < REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
   259	            await sleep(REVIEW_PROVIDER_RETRY_DELAY_MS * attempt);
   260	            continue;
   261	          }
   262	
   263	          throw statusError;
   264	        }
   265	
   266	        let parsedResponse: unknown = null;
   267	        try {
   268	          parsedResponse = bodyText ? JSON.parse(bodyText) : null;
   269	        } catch {
   270	          parsedResponse = null;
   271	        }
   272	
   273	        const content = parseOpenRouterContent(parsedResponse);
   274	        if (!content) {
   275	          throw new Error('Review analysis provider returned empty model content');
   276	        }
   277	        const actionPayload = JSON.parse(extractJsonObject(stripCodeFences(content))) as unknown;
   278	        const action = this.validateAction(actionPayload);
   279	        if (forceComplete && action.type !== 'complete') {
   280	          throw new Error('Review analysis finalization step must return type="complete"');
   281	        }
   282	        return action;
   283	      } catch (error) {
   284	        const timeoutLike = isTimeoutLikeError(error);
   285	        const transientNetworkError = error instanceof Error && /fetch failed|network|connection reset|econnreset|socket hang up/i.test(error.message);
   286	        if ((timeoutLike || transientNetworkError) && attempt < REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
   287	          await sleep(REVIEW_PROVIDER_RETRY_DELAY_MS * attempt);
   288	          continue;
   289	        }
   290	        if (timeoutLike) {
   291	          throw new Error(
   292	            `Review analysis provider request timed out after ${Math.floor(REVIEW_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)} seconds (attempt ${attempt}/${REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS})`
   293	          );
   294	        }
   295	        throw error;
   296	      } finally {
   297	        if (timeoutId !== null) {
   298	          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
   299	        }
   300	      }
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    throw new Error('Review analysis provider exhausted retry attempts without a valid response');
   304	  }
   305	}
   306	
   307	export class CloudflareAgentSdkReviewProvider implements ReviewAgentProvider {
   308	  constructor(
   309	    private readonly endpoint: string,
   310	    private readonly authToken: string | null,
   311	    private readonly serviceBinding: Fetcher | null,
   312	    private readonly openrouterApiKey: string | null,
   313	    private readonly validateAction: (action: unknown) => ReviewAgentAction
   314	  ) {}
   315	
   316	  async next(input: {
   317	    prompt: string;
   318	    model: string;
   319	    reasoningEffort?: 'minimal' | 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
   320	    maxSteps: number;
   321	    step: number;
   322	    history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[];
   323	    forceComplete?: boolean;
   324	  }): Promise<ReviewAgentAction> {
   325	    const requestFetch = this.serviceBinding ? this.serviceBinding.fetch.bind(this.serviceBinding) : fetch;
   326	
   327	    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
   328	      const controller = new AbortController();
   329	      let timeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
   330	
   331	      try {
   332	        const requestPromise = requestFetch(this.endpoint, {
   333	          method: 'POST',
   334	          headers: {
   335	            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   336	            ...(this.authToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${this.authToken}` } : {}),
   337	            ...(this.openrouterApiKey ? { 'X-Openrouter-Api-Key': this.openrouterApiKey } : {}),
   338	          },
   339	          body: JSON.stringify({
   340	            mode: 'workspace_task',
   341	            prompt: input.prompt,
   342	            model: input.model,
   343	            maxSteps: input.maxSteps,
   344	            step: input.step,
   345	            history: input.history,
   346	          }),
   347	          signal: controller.signal,
   348	        });
   349	
   350	        const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
   351	          timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
   352	            controller.abort('review_provider_timeout');
   353	            reject(new Error(`Review analysis provider request timed out after ${Math.floor(REVIEW_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)} seconds`));
   354	          }, REVIEW_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS);
   355	        });
   356	
   357	        const response = await Promise.race([requestPromise, timeoutPromise]);
   358	        if (!response.ok) {
   359	          const providerErrorBody = sanitizeErrorMessage(await response.text(), { openrouterApiKey: this.openrouterApiKey });
   360	          if (isMissingOpenRouterApiKeyError(providerErrorBody)) {
   361	            throw new Error(
   362	              'Review analysis provider is missing an OpenRouter API key. Configure OPENROUTER_API_KEY for nimbus-agent-endpoint or pass X-Openrouter-Api-Key from the caller.'
   363	            );
   364	          }
   365	          if (isWorkerToWorkerFetchRestriction(response.status, providerErrorBody)) {
   366	            throw new Error(
   367	              `Review analysis provider request blocked by Cloudflare Worker-to-Worker fetch restriction (error code 1042). Enable the 'global_fetch_strictly_public' compatibility flag on this worker or switch to a service binding.${providerErrorBody ? ` Provider response: ${providerErrorBody}` : ''}`
   368	            );
   369	          }
   370	
   371	          const transientStatus = response.status >= 500 || response.status === 429;
   372	          const statusError = new Error(
   373	            transientStatus
   374	              ? `Review analysis provider temporarily unavailable (status ${response.status})${providerErrorBody ? `: ${providerErrorBody}` : ''}`
   375	              : `Review analysis provider request failed with status ${response.status}${providerErrorBody ? `: ${providerErrorBody}` : ''}`
   376	          );
   377	
   378	          if (transientStatus && attempt < REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
   379	            await sleep(REVIEW_PROVIDER_RETRY_DELAY_MS * attempt);
   380	            continue;
   381	          }
   382	
   383	          throw statusError;
   384	        }
   385	
   386	        const parsed = (await response.json()) as unknown;
   387	        const action = asRecord(parsed).action;
   388	        const validatedAction = this.validateAction(action);
   389	        if (input.forceComplete === true && validatedAction.type !== 'complete') {
   390	          throw new Error('Review analysis finalization step must return type="complete"');
   391	        }
   392	        return validatedAction;
   393	      } catch (error) {
   394	        const timeoutLike = isTimeoutLikeError(error);
   395	        const transientNetworkError = error instanceof Error && /fetch failed|network|connection reset|econnreset|socket hang up/i.test(error.message);
   396	        if ((timeoutLike || transientNetworkError) && attempt < REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
   397	          await sleep(REVIEW_PROVIDER_RETRY_DELAY_MS * attempt);
   398	          continue;
   399	        }
   400	        if (timeoutLike) {
   401	          throw new Error(
   402	            `Review analysis provider request timed out after ${Math.floor(REVIEW_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)} seconds (attempt ${attempt}/${REVIEW_PROVIDER_MAX_ATTEMPTS})`
   403	          );
   404	        }
   405	        throw error;
   406	      } finally {
   407	        if (timeoutId !== null) {
   408	          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
   409	        }
   410	      }
   411	    }
   412	
   413	    throw new Error('Review analysis provider exhausted retry attempts without a valid response');
   414	  }
   415	}

import {
  AgentEndpointError,
  nextAgentActionWithInference,
  type AgentEnv,
  type AgentRequest,
} from './lib/agent.js';

const SENSITIVE_PATTERNS: RegExp[] = [
  /Bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~+/=-]+/gi,
  /\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\b/gi,
  /\bnmb_live_[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\b/gi,
];

const GENERIC_UPSTREAM_ERROR_MESSAGE = 'upstream error';

function readBearerToken(value: string | null): string | null {
  if (!value) {
    return null;
  }
  const match = value.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i);
  if (!match) {
    return null;
  }
  const token = match[1]?.trim();
  return token ? token : null;
}

function hasSensitivePattern(value: string): boolean {
  return SENSITIVE_PATTERNS.some((pattern) => {
    pattern.lastIndex = 0;
    return pattern.test(value);
  });
}

function sanitizeErrorMessage(rawMessage: string): string {
  const trimmed = rawMessage.trim();
  if (!trimmed) {
    return GENERIC_UPSTREAM_ERROR_MESSAGE;
  }

  let sanitized = trimmed;
  for (const pattern of SENSITIVE_PATTERNS) {
    pattern.lastIndex = 0;
    sanitized = sanitized.replace(pattern, '[REDACTED]');
  }

  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!sanitized || sanitized === '[REDACTED]' || hasSensitivePattern(sanitized)) {
    return GENERIC_UPSTREAM_ERROR_MESSAGE;
  }

  return sanitized;
}

function sanitizeErrorDetails(details: Record<string, unknown> | undefined): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (!details) {
    return null;
  }

  const message = details.message;
  if (typeof message !== 'string') {
    return details;
  }

  return {
    ...details,
    message: sanitizeErrorMessage(message),
  };
}

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: AgentEnv): Promise<Response> {
    if (request.method !== 'POST') {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'method_not_allowed' }), {
        status: 405,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const expectedAuthToken = (env.AGENT_SDK_AUTH_TOKEN ?? '').trim();
    const providedAuthToken = readBearerToken(request.headers.get('Authorization'));
    if (!expectedAuthToken || !providedAuthToken || providedAuthToken !== expectedAuthToken) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'unauthorized' }), {
        status: 401,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    let payload: AgentRequest;
    try {
      payload = (await request.json()) as AgentRequest;
    } catch {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'invalid_json' }), {
        status: 400,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    try {
      const openrouterApiKeyHeader = request.headers.get('X-Openrouter-Api-Key');
      const action = await nextAgentActionWithInference(payload, env, {
        openrouterApiKey: typeof openrouterApiKeyHeader === 'string' ? openrouterApiKeyHeader : null,
      });
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ action }), {
        status: 200,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof AgentEndpointError) {
        const safeDetails = sanitizeErrorDetails(error.details);
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            error: error.code,
            details: safeDetails,
          }),
          {
            status: error.status,
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          }
        );
      }

      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          error: 'internal_error',
          details: error instanceof Error ? { message: sanitizeErrorMessage(error.message) } : null,
        }),
        {
          status: 500,
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        }
      );
    }
  },
};

---AGENT LIB---
import { z } from 'zod';

type AgentHistoryEntry =
  | { role: 'assistant'; content: string }
  | { role: 'tool'; tool: string; output: unknown };

export type AgentAction =
  | { type: 'tool'; tool: 'list_files'; args: { path?: string } }
  | { type: 'tool'; tool: 'read_file'; args: { path: string; maxBytes?: number } }
  | { type: 'tool'; tool: 'diff_summary'; args: { maxBytes?: number } }
  | { type: 'final'; summary: string };

export interface AgentRequest {
  mode?: string;
  prompt?: string;
  model?: string;
  maxSteps?: number;
  step?: number;
  history?: AgentHistoryEntry[];
}

export interface AgentEnv {
  OPENROUTER_API_KEY?: string;
  DEFAULT_MODEL?: string;
  AGENT_SDK_AUTH_TOKEN?: string;
  OPENROUTER_HTTP_REFERER?: string;
  OPENROUTER_X_TITLE?: string;
}

export class AgentEndpointError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly code: string,
    public readonly status: number,
    public readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>
  ) {
    super(code);
    this.name = 'AgentEndpointError';
  }
}

export interface ReviewOutputV2 {
  findings: unknown[];
  summary: string;
  furtherPassesLowYield: boolean;
}

const REVIEW_FINDING_SEVERITIES = ['info', 'low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical'] as const;
const REVIEW_FINDING_CATEGORIES = ['security', 'logic', 'style', 'breaking-change'] as const;

const reviewLocationSchema = z
  .object({
    filePath: z.string().transform((value) => value.trim().replaceAll('\\', '/')).pipe(z.string().min(1)),
    startLine: z.number().int().positive().nullable(),
    endLine: z.number().int().positive().nullable(),
  })
  .superRefine((value, ctx) => {
    const startLine = value.startLine;
    const endLine = value.endLine;
    const hasNullPair = startLine === null && endLine === null;
    const hasIntegerPair = startLine !== null && endLine !== null && Number.isInteger(startLine) && Number.isInteger(endLine);
    if (!hasNullPair && !hasIntegerPair) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [], message: 'startLine/endLine must both be null or both be positive integers' });
      return;
    }
    if (hasIntegerPair && endLine < startLine) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [], message: 'endLine must be greater than or equal to startLine' });
    }
  });

function isValidationLikeFinding(text: string): boolean {
  return /\b(regex|normalize|normalization|validate|validation|pattern)\b/i.test(text);
}

function hasConcreteSampleAndOutcomeEvidence(failingScenario: string, evidence: string): boolean {
  const combined = `${failingScenario}\n${evidence}`;
  const hasConcreteSample =
    /\b(input|sample|string|value)\b/i.test(combined) || /`[^`]+`|'[^']+'|"[^"]+"/.test(combined);
  const hasOutcome = /\b(match|matches|reject|rejected|accept|accepted|return|returns|result|status|passes|fails)\b/i.test(
    combined
  );
  return hasConcreteSample && hasOutcome;
}

function isTimeoutBoundaryLikeFinding(text: string): boolean {
  return /\b(timeout|retry|deadline|interval|boundary|poll)\b/i.test(text);
}

function hasBoundaryAndStatusEvidence(failingScenario: string, evidence: string): boolean {
  const combined = `${failingScenario}\n${evidence}`;
  const hasBoundary = /\b\d+\b|>=|<=|>|<|==|\b(deadline|interval|timeout|ms|second|seconds)\b/i.test(combined);
  const hasStatusOutcome = /\b(status|queued|running|succeeded|failed|cancelled|return|returns|result)\b/i.test(combined);
  return hasBoundary && hasStatusOutcome;
}

const reviewFindingSchema = z
  .object({
    severity: z.enum(REVIEW_FINDING_SEVERITIES),
    category: z.enum(REVIEW_FINDING_CATEGORIES),
    passType: z.literal('single'),
    locations: z.array(reviewLocationSchema).min(1),
    description: z.string().transform((value) => value.trim()).pipe(z.string().min(1)),
    suggestedFix: z.string().transform((value) => value.trim()).pipe(z.string().min(1)),
    failingScenario: z.string().transform((value) => value.trim()).pipe(z.string().min(1)),
    evidence: z.string().transform((value) => value.trim()).pipe(z.string().min(1)),
    guardGap: z.string().transform((value) => value.trim()).pipe(z.string().min(1)),
  })
  .superRefine((value, ctx) => {
    const behaviorText = `${value.description}\n${value.suggestedFix}\n${value.failingScenario}`;
    if (isValidationLikeFinding(behaviorText) && !hasConcreteSampleAndOutcomeEvidence(value.failingScenario, value.evidence)) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: 'custom',
        message: 'validation/regex findings require concrete sample input and observed outcome in failingScenario/evidence',
        path: ['evidence'],
      });
    }
    if (isTimeoutBoundaryLikeFinding(behaviorText) && !hasBoundaryAndStatusEvidence(value.failingScenario, value.evidence)) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: 'custom',
        message: 'timeout/retry findings require explicit boundary values and resulting status in failingScenario/evidence',
        path: ['evidence'],
      });
    }
  });

const reviewOutputV2Schema = z.object({
  findings: z.array(reviewFindingSchema),
  summary: z.string().transform((value) => value.trim()).pipe(z.string().min(1)),
  furtherPassesLowYield: z.boolean(),
});

const reviewOutputV2JsonSchema = {
  name: 'ReviewAnalysisOutputV2',
  strict: true,
  schema: {
    type: 'object',
    additionalProperties: false,
    properties: {
      findings: {
        type: 'array',
        items: {
          type: 'object',
          additionalProperties: false,
          properties: {
            severity: { type: 'string', enum: [...REVIEW_FINDING_SEVERITIES] },
            category: { type: 'string', enum: [...REVIEW_FINDING_CATEGORIES] },
            passType: { type: 'string', enum: ['single'] },
            locations: {
              type: 'array',
              items: {
                type: 'object',
                additionalProperties: false,
                properties: {
                  filePath: { type: 'string' },
                  startLine: { anyOf: [{ type: 'integer' }, { type: 'null' }] },
                  endLine: { anyOf: [{ type: 'integer' }, { type: 'null' }] },
                },
                required: ['filePath', 'startLine', 'endLine'],
              },
            },
            description: { type: 'string' },
            suggestedFix: { type: 'string' },
            failingScenario: { type: 'string' },
            evidence: { type: 'string' },
            guardGap: { type: 'string' },
          },
          required: [
            'severity',
            'category',
            'passType',
            'locations',
            'description',
            'suggestedFix',
            'failingScenario',
            'evidence',
            'guardGap',
          ],
        },
      },
      summary: { type: 'string' },
      furtherPassesLowYield: { type: 'boolean' },
    },
    required: ['findings', 'summary', 'furtherPassesLowYield'],
  },
} as const;

function hasToolOutput(history: AgentHistoryEntry[], tool: string): boolean {
  return history.some((entry) => entry.role === 'tool' && entry.tool === tool);
}

function isReviewPrompt(prompt: string): boolean {
  const normalized = prompt.toLowerCase();
  return (
    normalized.includes('you are nimbus review') ||
    normalized.includes('furtherpasseslowyield') ||
    normalized.includes('return your final answer as raw json')
  );
}

function stripCodeFences(raw: string): string {
  const trimmed = raw.trim();
  const match = trimmed.match(/^```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```$/i);
  return match ? match[1].trim() : trimmed;
}

function extractJsonObjectCandidate(raw: string): string | null {
  const source = raw.trim();
  const first = source.indexOf('{');
  if (first < 0) {
    return null;
  }

  let depth = 0;
  let inString = false;
  let escaped = false;
  for (let i = first; i < source.length; i += 1) {
    const ch = source[i];
    if (inString) {
      if (escaped) {
        escaped = false;
      } else if (ch === '\\') {
        escaped = true;
      } else if (ch === '"') {
        inString = false;
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (ch === '"') {
      inString = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '{') {
      depth += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '}') {
      depth -= 1;
      if (depth === 0) {
        return source.slice(first, i + 1).trim();
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function resolveOpenRouterModel(requestModel: string | undefined, defaultModel: string | undefined): string {
  const raw = (typeof requestModel === 'string' ? requestModel : '').trim() || (defaultModel ?? '').trim();
  if (!raw) {
    return 'anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-5';
  }
  if (raw === 'sonnet-4.5') {
    return 'anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-5';
  }
  return raw;
}

function parseOpenRouterContent(payload: unknown): string {
  const record = payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload) ? (payload as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
  const choices = Array.isArray(record?.choices) ? record.choices : [];
  const first = choices[0];
  const choiceRecord = first && typeof first === 'object' && !Array.isArray(first) ? (first as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
  const message = choiceRecord?.message;
  const messageRecord = message && typeof message === 'object' && !Array.isArray(message)
    ? (message as Record<string, unknown>)
    : null;
  const content = messageRecord?.content;
  if (typeof content === 'string') {
    return content.trim();
  }
  if (Array.isArray(content)) {
    const joined = content
      .map((part) => {
        if (!part || typeof part !== 'object' || Array.isArray(part)) {
          return '';
        }
        const text = (part as Record<string, unknown>).text;
        return typeof text === 'string' ? text : '';
      })
      .join('')
      .trim();
    return joined;
  }
  return '';
}

export async function callOpenRouter(input: {
  apiKey: string;
  model: string;
  prompt: string;
  httpReferer?: string;
  xTitle?: string;
}): Promise<string> {
  let response: Response;
  let timeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  try {
    const signal =
      typeof AbortSignal.timeout === 'function'
        ? AbortSignal.timeout(90_000)
        : (() => {
            const controller = new AbortController();
            timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort('openrouter_timeout'), 90_000);
            return controller.signal;
          })();
    response = await fetch('https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        Authorization: `Bearer ${input.apiKey}`,
        ...(input.httpReferer ? { 'HTTP-Referer': input.httpReferer } : {}),
        ...(input.xTitle ? { 'X-Title': input.xTitle } : {}),
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: input.model,
        response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: reviewOutputV2JsonSchema },
        plugins: [{ id: 'response-healing' }],
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: input.prompt }],
      }),
      signal,
    });

+              furtherPassesLowYield: true,
+            },
+          },
+        }),
+      } as Response;
+    }) as typeof fetch;
+
+    const provider = new CloudflareAgentSdkReviewProvider(
+      'https://example.com/agent',
+      null,
+      null,
+      null,
+      validateReviewAgentAction
+    );
+
+    const result = await provider.next({
+      prompt: 'test prompt',
+      model: 'gpt-5.4',
+      reasoningEffort: 'high',
+      maxSteps: 8,
+      step: 2,
+      history: [],
+      forceComplete: true,
+    });
+
+    assert.equal(result.type, 'complete');
+    assert.equal(capturedBody?.model, 'gpt-5.4');
+    assert.equal(capturedBody?.reasoningEffort, 'high');
+    assert.equal(capturedBody?.forceComplete, true);
+  } finally {
+    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
+  }
 }

{"findings":[{"title":"[P1] Fence finalization by attempt before accepting recovered output","body":"When `recoverStaleRunningReviewIfNeeded()` requeues a slow review, the original worker is still running. This guard only checks `latest.status === 'running'`, so once the replacement attempt is claimed and flips the row back to `running`, the stale original attempt can still persist `succeeded` findings over the newer run; if the retry has not been claimed yet, the same guard drops the finished report entirely. Finalization needs to be fenced by the attempt that produced the report, not just the current status.","confidence_score":0.98,"priority":1,"code_location":{"absolute_file_path":"/Users/nickdejesus/Code/nimbus/packages/worker/src/lib/review-runner/finalization.ts","line_range":{"start":27,"end":29}}},{"title":"[P1] Treat manually failed reviews as terminal before retry scheduling","body":"This new path marks a still-running review as `failed` with `review_execution_aborted`, but it does not stop the in-flight worker. If that attempt later hits a transient error (for example a provider timeout or network reset), `processReviewRun()` will still call `scheduleReviewRetry()` and move the review back to `queued`, so the new “Fail review” action does not actually stop the run cleanly. The worker needs to recognize `review_execution_aborted` as a terminal fence before retry/finalization logic runs.","confidence_score":0.96,"priority":1,"code_location":{"absolute_file_path":"/Users/nickdejesus/Code/nimbus/packages/worker/src/api/reviews/recovery.ts","line_range":{"start":297,"end":302}}},{"title":"[P2] Send repair flags to AGENT_SDK review requests","body":"The hardened repair flow only works if the second provider call tells the remote agent it is a forced-completion repair turn, but the `AGENT_SDK_URL` branch still posts only `prompt`/`model`/`step`/`history`. In self-hosted deployments that use the Cloudflare agent endpoint instead of direct OpenRouter, a schema-invalid first completion is retried as an ordinary step with no `forceComplete` hint, so the new bounded repair behavior (and `REVIEW_REASONING_EFFORT`) is silently ineffective there.","confidence_score":0.92,"priority":2,"code_location":{"absolute_file_path":"/Users/nickdejesus/Code/nimbus/packages/worker/src/lib/review-analysis/provider.ts","line_range":{"start":339,"end":345}}}],"overall_correctness":"patch is incorrect","overall_explanation":"The patch introduces real hardening, but it still leaves stale and manually failed executions insufficiently fenced, and it does not wire the new repair controls through the AGENT_SDK path. Those cases can overwrite or resurrect reviews and prevent the new repair loop from working in one supported deployment mode.","overall_confidence_score":0.96}
